- **cmd-account**: - `account add` — Add a new account to 1Password CLI manually using your account password and Secret Key, instead of signing in via the 1Password app. - `account forget` — Remove a 1Password account from this device. - `account get` — Get details about your account. - `account list` — List users and accounts set up on this device. ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-account.md))
- **cmd-activate**: Outputs shell code that activates mise in the current shell session. When evaluated, it hooks into the prompt to keep PATH and environment variables updated as you change directories. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-activate.md))
- **cmd-add**: Adds target files to the chezmoi source state. If a target is already managed, its source state is replaced with the current state from the destination directory. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-add.md))
- **cmd-add-fpath**: Add a plugin's directory (or a subdirectory of it) to `$fpath` without loading or sourcing the plugin. Use this to expose completions or autoload functions without running any plugin code. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-add-fpath.md))
- **cmd-agent-list**: List all SDK-enabled agents ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/core/commands/cmd-agent-list.md))
- **cmd-agent-run**: Run an agent with Claude Agent SDK ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/core/commands/cmd-agent-run.md))
- **cmd-agent-show**: Show SDK definition for an agent ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/core/commands/cmd-agent-show.md))
- **cmd-alpha**: First command ([DylanLIiii/Aone](https://github.com/DylanLIiii/Aone/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/claude_typical/.claude/commands/cmd-alpha.md))
- **cmd-apply**: Ensures that target files match the desired target state defined in the source directory, updating them as necessary. This is the primary command for applying your dotfile configuration to the current machine. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-apply.md))
- **cmd-archive**: Generates a tar or zip archive of the computed target state. Useful for inspecting what chezmoi would apply, creating backups, or transferring dotfiles to systems without chezmoi. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-archive.md))

- **cmd-backlog-diff**: Compare local tickets against Jira remote state ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-diff.md))
- **cmd-backlog-import**: Import tickets from Jira ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-import.md))

- **cmd-backlog-pull**: Pull tickets from Jira to local backlog files ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-pull.md))
- **cmd-backlog-push**: Push local ticket changes to Jira ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-push.md))
- **cmd-backlog-update-fields**: Update ticket field mappings ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-update-fields.md))
- **cmd-backlog-validate**: Validate ticket files against v10.1.1 standards ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/backlog/commands/cmd-backlog-validate.md))
- **cmd-beta**: Second command ([DylanLIiii/Aone](https://github.com/DylanLIiii/Aone/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/claude_typical/.claude/commands/cmd-beta.md))
- **cmd-bindkeys**: List all bindkeys set up by each loaded plugin. Use this to audit key bindings and identify conflicts between plugins. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-bindkeys.md))
- **cmd-both-off**: A command. ([localixai/agent-plugins](https://github.com/localixai/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/spec/oracle/runtime/invocation-flags/commands/cmd-both-off.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cmd-break**: Aliases: `worst-case`, `break-ui`. **Posture: adversary, then judge.** Build the worst-case data and the toggle, report, and stop. Never fix the component until the user names which breaks to fix. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/design-engineering/references/commands/cmd-break.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
- **cmd-cache**: `uv cache` provides subcommands to inspect, clean, and optimise uv's on-disk cache. The cache stores downloaded wheels, source distributions, and built artifacts so subsequent installs avoid redundant network requests and rebuilds. ([turboBasic/uv-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/uv-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cache.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-cat**: Writes the computed target contents of files, scripts, or symlinks to stdout. Useful for previewing what chezmoi would write without actually applying. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cat.md))
- **cmd-cclear**: Clear stray and improper completions from Zinit's completions directory. Use this to fix broken or dangling completion symlinks. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cclear.md))
- **cmd-cd**: Launches a subshell in the chezmoi working tree (source directory), optionally at the source path corresponding to a given target path. Useful for quick edits to source state files. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cd.md))
- **cmd-cd**: Change the current directory to a plugin's local directory. Also supports snippets when given their URL. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cd.md))
- **cmd-cdclear**: Clear the list of intercepted `compdef` calls, discarding any completions captured so far. Use this to ignore completions registered by plugins loaded before this point. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdclear.md))
- **cmd-cdisable**: Disable a specific installed completion without uninstalling it. The completion file remains on disk but is removed from the active `$fpath` symlinks. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdisable.md))
- **cmd-cdlist**: Show the list of `compdef` calls that have been intercepted and are waiting to be replayed. Use this to inspect what completions have been captured before `compinit` ran. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdlist.md))
- **cmd-cdreplay**: Replay all `compdef` calls that were intercepted by Zinit before `compinit` was available. Run this once after calling `compinit` to register completions that plugins tried to define earlier. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdreplay.md))
- **cmd-cenable**: Re-enable a completion that was previously disabled with `zi cdisable`. Restores the symlink so the completion is active for `compinit`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cenable.md))
- **cmd-changes**: View the git log for a plugin's repository. Shows the commit history to review recent changes after an update. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-changes.md))
- **cmd-chattr**: Changes the attributes and/or type of targets in the source state by renaming source files to add or remove attribute prefixes. This is the primary way to toggle properties like `executable`, `private`, `template`, `encrypted`, etc. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-chattr.md))
- **cmd-commit**: You are a git commit specialist that creates well-structured conventional commits following the standard format. When this command is run, you will: ([elijahmurray/vandra-dev](https://github.com/elijahmurray/vandra-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/cmd-commit.md) · ★2)
- **cmd-compile**: Compile a plugin's Zsh scripts to `.zwc` bytecode to speed up loading. Can target a single plugin or all managed plugins at once. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-compile.md))
- **cmd-compiled**: List all plugins that currently have compiled `.zwc` bytecode files. Use this to audit which plugins benefit from compilation. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-compiled.md))
- **cmd-compinit**: Refresh installed completions by re-running the completion system initialization. A convenience wrapper around `autoload compinit; compinit`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-compinit.md))
- **cmd-completion**: - Bash: add to `.bashrc`; also requires the `bash-completion` package. - Zsh: add to `.zshrc`. - fish: add to your `.fish` file. - PowerShell: add to your `.ps1` file; requires script execution to be enabled. ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-completion.md))
- **cmd-completions**: List all completions currently installed and managed by Zinit, displayed in a multi-column layout. Also available as `zi clist`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-completions.md))
- **cmd-confluence-create-page**: Create and publish a Confluence page ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/confluence/commands/cmd-confluence-create-page.md))
- **cmd-confluence-fetch-page**: Fetch Confluence page content ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/confluence/commands/cmd-confluence-fetch-page.md))
- **cmd-confluence-import-reports**: Import reports to Confluence ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/confluence/commands/cmd-confluence-import-reports.md))
- **cmd-confluence-validate**: Validate markdown for ADF conversion ([eligoss/cc-backlog-manager](https://github.com/eligoss/cc-backlog-manager/blob/HEAD/framework/modules/confluence/commands/cmd-confluence-validate.md))
- **cmd-connect**: - `connect group` — manage group access to Secrets Automation: - `connect group grant` / `connect group revoke` — grant or revoke a group's access. Flags: `--all-servers` (all current and future servers), `--group group`, `--server server`. If no server is specified on grant, the group is added to the list of Secrets Automation managers. - `connect server` — manage Connect servers: - `connect serv ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-connect.md))
- **cmd-council**: Build posture with a judge panel: you fix, the judges only score. The target is a screen, a component, a page, a faux UI or a film; without one, use the change in front of you. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/design-engineering/references/commands/cmd-council.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
- **cmd-create**: Create a new plugin skeleton in the Zinit plugins directory, optionally also creating a matching GitHub repository. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-create.md))
- **cmd-create-component**: Scaffold a new Agilearn feature: a component under `src/features/&lt;name&gt;/` plus its TanStack Query hook in `src/lib/queries/`. No Zustand, no Zod, no i18n — Agilearn uses none of those. Server state is TanStack Query; keys come from `keys.ts`. ([azialah/agilearn](https://github.com/azialah/agilearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cmd-create-component.md))
- **cmd-create-migration**: Scaffold a new Supabase Postgres migration for Agilearn with RLS wired through the project's helper functions. ([azialah/agilearn](https://github.com/azialah/agilearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cmd-create-migration.md))
- **cmd-creinstall**: Install completions for a plugin without loading the plugin itself. Use this to set up completions from a local directory or a plugin that has already been downloaded. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-creinstall.md))
- **cmd-csearch**: Search all downloaded plugin directories for available completion files, whether or not those completions are currently installed. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-csearch.md))
- **cmd-cuninstall**: Uninstall (remove) completions for a plugin from Zinit's completions directory. The plugin itself is not deleted. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cuninstall.md))
- **cmd-data**: Writes the computed template data to stdout in JSON or YAML format. Use this to inspect what variables are available in templates (`.chezmoi.*` and user-defined data). ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-data.md))
- **cmd-dclear**: Clear the session investigation report without reverting any changes. Resets the tracking data accumulated since `zi dstart`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dclear.md))
- **cmd-delete**: Remove a plugin or snippet from disk. Use this to free disk space or to forget an incorrectly configured plugin so it can be cleanly re-installed. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-delete.md))
- **cmd-destroy**: Permanently removes a target from the source state, the destination directory, and the chezmoi state database. This is a destructive operation that deletes files from both your home directory and the source directory. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-destroy.md))
- **cmd-diff**: Prints the difference between the target state (what chezmoi wants) and the destination state (what currently exists on disk). Use this to preview what `chezmoi apply` would change. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-diff.md))
- **cmd-dispatch-subagent**: Hand one cohesive deliverable to one subagent with everything it needs and nothing it does not, so that its output can be validated and integrated without guessing. ([dradoaica/cogov-a-sdlc](https://github.com/dradoaica/cogov-a-sdlc/blob/HEAD/.agents/commands/cmd-dispatch-subagent.md) · ★3)
- **cmd-docs**: Generate and serve the docs for your dbt project. ([dbt-labs/docs.getdbt.com](https://github.com/dbt-labs/docs.getdbt.com/blob/HEAD/website/docs/reference/commands/cmd-docs.md) · ★223)
- **cmd-docs**: `dbt docs` has two supported subcommands: `generate` and `serve`. ([three-cubes/kairix](https://github.com/three-cubes/kairix/blob/HEAD/reference-library/data-and-analysis/dbt-docs/website/docs/reference/commands/cmd-docs.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-doctor**: Runs a series of checks to detect potential problems with the chezmoi installation, configuration, and environment. First command to run when troubleshooting. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-doctor.md))
- **cmd-doctor**: Displays diagnostic information about your mise installation and detects potential issues. Include its output in bug reports. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-doctor.md))
- **cmd-document** ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-document.md))
- **cmd-dreport**: Print a report of everything that happened in the current interactive session between `dstart`/`dtrace` and `dstop`. Shows aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other changes made during the tracked period. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dreport.md))
- **cmd-dstart**: Start investigating the current interactive session — Zinit begins recording all aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other shell-state changes from this point forward. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dstart.md))
- **cmd-dstop**: Stop the active session investigation started with `zi dstart`/`zi dtrace`. No further changes are recorded after this point. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dstop.md))
- **cmd-dump**: Dumps the full target state (type, permissions, contents, etc.) as JSON or YAML. Shows the complete metadata chezmoi has computed for targets, not just the file contents. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dump.md))
- **cmd-dunload**: Revert all shell-state changes recorded between `zi dstart` and `zi dstop`. Undoes aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other modifications captured during the session investigation window. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dunload.md))
- **cmd-edit**: Opens the source state of target files in your editor. Handles encrypted file decryption/re-encryption transparently and can optionally apply changes immediately after editing. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-edit.md))
- **cmd-edit**: Open a plugin's main file in `$EDITOR`. A quick way to inspect or patch a plugin's source without navigating to its directory manually. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-edit.md))
- **cmd-env**: Outputs environment variables that mise would set. Useful for debugging, non-interactive shells, and integration with other tools. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-env.md))
- **cmd-env-whitelist**: Specify environment variable names (or patterns) that should not be reverted when a plugin is unloaded. Use this to preserve variables that plugins set and that you want to keep after unloading. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-env-whitelist.md))
- **cmd-environment**: The `--environments` flag requires the latest beta build of 1Password CLI, version `2.33.0-beta.02` or later. ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-environment.md))

- **cmd-review**: **Posture: judge.** Read and report. Never edit code unless the user asks after the table. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/design-engineering/references/commands/cmd-review.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
- **cmd-review**: Review an existing slash command from the current project. ([wombat9000/claude-plugins](https://github.com/wombat9000/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/config-wizard/commands/cmd-review.md) · ★3)
- **cmd-rls-audit**: Verify every table has RLS enabled with working policies. In Agilearn, RLS is the **only** access-control boundary (anon-key SPA, no backend) — this audit is the security review. ([azialah/agilearn](https://github.com/azialah/agilearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cmd-rls-audit.md))
- **cmd-run**: `uv run` executes a command or script inside the project's virtual environment, first ensuring that environment is up-to-date with the lockfile. It is the primary way to run project code without manually activating `.venv`. ([turboBasic/uv-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/uv-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-run.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-run**: Flags: ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-run.md))
- **cmd-run**: Run a task defined in `mise.toml` or as a file task. Tasks execute with the full mise context (tools + env vars) loaded, and dependencies run in parallel by default. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-run.md))
- **cmd-run**: Run an arbitrary shell command inside a plugin's directory. Useful for executing plugin-provided scripts or tools without changing your current directory. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-run.md))
- **cmd-security-check**: Pre-deploy security pass for Agilearn. The threat surface is small: an anon-key SPA where RLS does all access control and a private storage bucket holds uploads. This checklist targets exactly those. ([azialah/agilearn](https://github.com/azialah/agilearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cmd-security-check.md))
- **cmd-self**: `uv self` is the subcommand group for managing the uv binary itself. It provides `uv self update` to upgrade (or downgrade) the uv executable and `uv self version` to display uv's own version string, including build commit and date. ([turboBasic/uv-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/uv-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-self.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-self-update**: Updates mise itself to the latest version. The simplest way to keep mise current. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-self-update.md))
- **cmd-self-update**: Update and recompile Zinit itself. Run this after installing Zinit or whenever you want to pull the latest Zinit source. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-self-update.md))
- **cmd-service-account**: - `service-account create` — Create a service account to gain programmatic access to your secrets using 1Password CLI. - `service-account ratelimit` — Retrieve hourly and daily rate limit usage for a service account. - **cmd-settings**: View and modify mise settings. Settings control mise's own behavior (not project tools or env vars). ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-settings.md))
- **cmd-setup-ticket-system**: Guide the user through configuring their ticket management system in CLAUDE.md. ([elijahmurray/vandra-dev](https://github.com/elijahmurray/vandra-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/cmd-setup-ticket-system.md) · ★2)
- **cmd-shell**: Sets a tool version for the current shell session only. Does not modify any config files — the version is active until the shell exits. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-shell.md))
- **cmd-ship**: Aliases: `prep`, `prod`, `launch`. **Posture: fix.** Edit the code, then report. With `audit`, judge only: every row's status is Open. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/design-engineering/references/commands/cmd-ship.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)

- **cmd-snippet**: Source a local or remote file directly by URL. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-snippet.md))

- **cmd-source-path**: Prints the source directory path corresponding to a target path. Without arguments, prints the root source directory. Useful for scripting and for navigating to source files. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-source-path.md))
- **cmd-srv**: Control a background service plugin — start, stop, restart, advance to the next Zsh instance, or quit it entirely. Services are plugins loaded with the `service` ice modifier. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-srv.md))
- **cmd-stage**: Build posture, except `review`, which judges and does not edit. ([AgentsORG/design-engineering](https://github.com/AgentsORG/design-engineering/blob/HEAD/skills/design-engineering/references/commands/cmd-stage.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
